    {
      "id": "kodinhaltija-c01",
      "source_id": "kodinhaltija-s1",
      "quote": "Kodinhaltija tai kodinhaltia eli kotitonttu on kansanperinteessä haltija tai tonttu, jonka uskotaan asuvan asuintalossa.",
      "locator": "johdanto",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kodinhaltija, kodinhaltia, atau kotitonttu dalam tradisi rakyat adalah haltija atau tonttu yang dipercaya tinggal di rumah hunian.",
        "en": "The kodinhaltija, kodinhaltia or kotitonttu is in folk tradition a haltija or tonttu believed to live in a dwelling house."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kodinhaltija-c02",
      "source_id": "kodinhaltija-s1",
      "quote": "Usein kodinhaltijan uskottiin tulevan taloon sillä hetkellä, kun taloon sytytetään ensi kertaa tuli. Toisinaan on uskottu, että kodinhaltijaksi on jäänyt ensimmäinen tulentekijä eli usein talon perustaja, tai talossa ensimmäisenä kuollut ihminen.",
      "locator": "johdanto",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kodinhaltija sering dipercaya datang ke rumah saat api pertama kali dinyalakan di sana; kadang dipercaya bahwa yang menjadi kodinhaltija adalah orang pertama yang menyalakan api, biasanya pendiri rumah, atau orang pertama yang meninggal di rumah itu.",
        "en": "The kodinhaltija was often believed to arrive when a fire was first lit in the house; sometimes it was believed that the first fire-maker, often the founder of the house, or the first person to die there, became the kodinhaltija."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kodinhaltija-c03",
      "source_id": "kodinhaltija-s1",
      "quote": "Kodinhaltijan on yleensä kerrottu viihtyvän tulisijan äärellä, uunin päällä tai uunin takana. Joskus sen on kuvailtu pamautelleen uunin luukkua merkiksi siitä, että joku tuli taloon.",
      "locator": "Ulkomuoto ja sijat",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kodinhaltija biasanya dikatakan senang berada di dekat perapian, di atas atau di belakang tungku, dan kadang membanting pintu tungku sebagai tanda ada orang yang datang.",
        "en": "The kodinhaltija is usually said to like being by the hearth, on top of or behind the stove, and sometimes banged the stove door as a sign that someone was coming to the house."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kodinhaltija-c04",
      "source_id": "kodinhaltija-s1",
      "quote": "Ruokapöydässä kodinhaltijalle annettiin usein oma osuutensa ennen muita ruokailijoita. Kodinhaltijan kanssa yritettiin pysyä hyvissä väleissä, sillä huonosti kohdeltu kodinhaltija alkoi häiriköidä. Haltijan näyttäytymistä pidettiin onnettomuuden tai kuoleman enteenä.",
      "locator": "Ylläpito tai sen puute ja hyvät suhteet",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di meja makan kodinhaltija sering diberi bagiannya sebelum yang lain; orang berusaha menjaga hubungan baik dengannya karena kodinhaltija yang diperlakukan buruk akan mulai mengganggu, dan penampakannya dianggap pertanda kecelakaan atau kematian.",
        "en": "At the table the kodinhaltija was often given its share before the other diners; people tried to stay on good terms with it, since a badly treated kodinhaltija began to cause trouble, and its appearance was taken as an omen of misfortune or death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kodinhaltija-c05",
      "source_id": "kodinhaltija-s1",
      "quote": "Kun kodinhaltijan haluttiin muuttavan uuteen taloon jos sellainen rakennettiin vanhan tilalle, tuotiin uuteen taloon vanhan talon tulisijan kiviä, tuhkaa tai multaa.",
      "locator": "Ylläpito tai sen puute ja hyvät suhteet",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jika kodinhaltija diharapkan pindah ke rumah baru yang dibangun menggantikan rumah lama, batu perapian, abu, atau tanah dari rumah lama dibawa ke rumah baru.",
        "en": "When the kodinhaltija was wanted to move into a new house built to replace the old one, stones, ash or soil from the old hearth were brought to the new house."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kratti adalah hantu yang berteriak dalam mitologi Finlandia, penjaga harta karun terpendam yang menurut catatan lama bermata satu dan memberi kekayaan kepada pemujanya.",
    "en": "Kratti is a shouting ghost of Finnish mythology, guardian of buried treasure, described in old records as one-eyed and granting wealth to its worshippers.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kratti-c01",

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Finlandia, Kratti atau Skratti adalah hantu yang berteriak dan berbaring di atas harta serta peti uang yang tersembunyi di dalam tanah, menguasai dan menjaganya. Aarni adalah makhluk serupa. Namanya berasal dari kata Swedia \"skratt\" yang berarti tawa. Mikael Agricola mencatatnya di antara roh penjaga yang dipuja orang Häme, dan menurut puisi Sigfridus Aronus Forsius dari abad ke-16 atau ke-17, Kratti bermata satu dan menganugerahkan kekayaan kepada pemujanya.",
      "en": "In Finnish mythology Kratti or Skratti is a shouting ghost that lies on treasures and money chests hidden in the ground, ruling and guarding them. Aarni is a similar being. Its name comes from the Swedish \"skratt\", meaning laughter. Mikael Agricola listed it among the guardian spirits worshipped by the people of Häme, and according to a poem by Sigfridus Aronus Forsius from the 1500s or 1600s, Kratti is one-eyed and grants wealth to its worshipper.",

  "images": [],

    {
      "id": "kratti-s1",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "kratti-c01",
      "source_id": "kratti-s1",
      "quote": "Kratti tai Skratti on suomalaisessa mytologiassa huutava kummitus, joka makaa maahan kätkettyjen aarteiden ja rahakirstujen päällä niitä halliten ja vartioiden.",
      "locator": "johdanto",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Finlandia, Kratti atau Skratti adalah hantu yang berteriak, berbaring di atas harta dan peti uang yang tersembunyi di dalam tanah, menguasai dan menjaganya.",
        "en": "In Finnish mythology Kratti or Skratti is a shouting ghost that lies on treasures and money chests hidden in the ground, ruling and guarding them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kratti-c02",
      "source_id": "kratti-s1",
      "quote": "Samantapainen on myös Aarni. Nimi Kratti tulee ruotsin sanasta skratt ’nauru’.",
      "locator": "johdanto",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aarni adalah makhluk serupa; nama Kratti berasal dari kata Swedia \"skratt\" (tawa).",
        "en": "Aarni is a similar being; the name Kratti comes from the Swedish word \"skratt\" (laughter)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kratti-c03",
      "source_id": "kratti-s1",
      "quote": "Mikael Agricolan mukaan Kratti kuului hämäläisten palvomiin haltijoihin.",
      "locator": "johdanto",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Mikael Agricola, Kratti termasuk roh penjaga (haltija) yang dipuja orang Häme.",
        "en": "According to Mikael Agricola, Kratti was among the guardian spirits (haltija) worshipped by the Tavastians (Häme people)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kratti-c04",
      "source_id": "kratti-s1",
      "quote": "Sigfridus Aronus Forsiuksen 1500- tai 1600-luvulla kirjoittaman runon mukaan Kratti on yksisilmäinen ja lahjoittaa rikkautta palvojalleen.",
      "locator": "johdanto",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut sebuah puisi Sigfridus Aronus Forsius dari abad ke-16 atau ke-17 (1500- atau 1600-an), Kratti bermata satu dan menganugerahkan kekayaan kepada pemujanya.",
        "en": "According to a poem by Sigfridus Aronus Forsius written in the 1500s or 1600s, Kratti is one-eyed and grants wealth to its worshipper."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Krepel atau Naga Bytom adalah naga legenda Polandia dari hutan dekat Bytom, Silesia Hulu, yang ramah dan menghadiahkan hartanya kepada seorang buruh tani.",
    "en": "Krepel, the Bytom Dragon, is a legendary Polish dragon from the forests near Bytom, Upper Silesia, a friendly creature that gave its treasure to a farmhand.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "krepel-mythology-c01",
      "krepel-mythology-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Krepel, juga disebut Naga Bytom, adalah naga legenda Polandia yang konon menghuni hutan dekat Bytom di Silesia Hulu. Berbeda dari Naga Wawel di Kraków, Krepel konon cukup \"sopan\". Para tetua setempat bercerita bahwa ia menjaga harta.",
      "en": "Krepel, also called the Bytom Dragon, is a legendary Polish dragon said to have lived in the forests near Bytom in Upper Silesia. Unlike the Wawel Dragon of Kraków, Krepel was said to be rather \"well-mannered\". Local elders told that it guarded a treasure.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "krepel-mythology-c01",
        "krepel-mythology-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut legenda, seorang buruh tani muda pergi untuk merebut harta itu dan bersiap melawan naga di depan gua dengan pentungan. Namun ia memilih memercayai Krepel, tinggal bersamanya untuk sementara, dan diberi sebagian hartanya. Dengan harta itu ia membeli rumah petak di tempat yang kini Jalan Piłsudski dan memasang gambar untuk menghormati Krepel.",
      "en": "According to legend, a young farmhand set out to claim the treasure and readied himself with a club before the dragon's grotto. Yet he chose to trust Krepel, stayed with it for a while and was given some of its treasure. With it he bought a tenement on what is now Piłsudski Street and put up an image in Krepel's honour.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "krepel-mythology-c02",
        "krepel-mythology-c03",
        "krepel-mythology-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kini Naga Bytom menjadi simbol ikonis kota dan sering muncul pada suvenir wisata. Pada 2023 terbit buku anak Bytom Fantastyczny karya Katarzyna Mołdawa yang menampilkan tokoh berdasarkan patung naga di Jalan Piłsudski.",
      "en": "Today the Bytom Dragon is an iconic symbol of the city and often appears on tourist souvenirs. In 2023 the children's book Bytom Fantastyczny by Katarzyna Mołdawa featured a character based on the dragon sculpture on Piłsudski Street.",

      "quote": "is a legendary Polish dragon said to have inhabited the forests near Bytom in Upper Silesia. Unlike its cracovian counterpart—the Wawel Dragon, Krepel is said to have been rather \"well-mannered\".",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Krepel, juga disebut Naga Bytom (smok bytomski), adalah naga legenda Polandia yang konon menghuni hutan dekat Bytom di Silesia Hulu; berbeda dari padanannya di Kraków, Naga Wawel, Krepel konon cukup \"sopan\".",
        "en": "Krepel, also called the Bytom Dragon (smok bytomski), is a legendary Polish dragon said to have lived in the forests near Bytom in Upper Silesia; unlike its Kraków counterpart, the Wawel Dragon, Krepel was said to be rather \"well-mannered\"."

    {
      "id": "krepel-mythology-c02",
      "source_id": "krepel-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "local elders spoke of a dragon guarding a treasure. Intrigued by these tales, a young farmhand decided to venture out to the borderland of Bytom land and claim the treasure for himself.",
      "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para tetua setempat bercerita tentang naga yang menjaga harta; tertarik oleh kisah itu, seorang buruh tani muda memutuskan pergi ke perbatasan tanah Bytom untuk merebut harta tersebut.",

      "id": "krepel-mythology-c03",
      "source_id": "krepel-mythology-s1",

      "source_id": "krepel-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Deciding to trust the creature, the farmhand set out for the dragon's residence, where they spent some time together.       "locator": "Legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Memutuskan memercayai makhluk itu, sang buruh tani pergi ke kediaman naga dan menghabiskan waktu bersamanya; setelah diberi sebagian harta naga, ia membeli rumah petak di tempat yang kini Jalan Piłsudski dan memasang gambar untuk menghormati sahabat barunya, Krepel.",
        "en": "Deciding to trust the creature, the farmhand went to the dragon's residence and spent time with it; given some of the dragon's treasure, he later bought a tenement on what is now Piłsudski Street and put up an image honouring his new friend, Krepel."

    "en": "Kuyva is a giant of Sami mythology who robbed and killed the Sami in the Lovozero tundra and was struck by the gods' lightning, leaving a silhouette on a cliff by Seydozero.",

    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Sami, Kuyva adalah raksasa legenda yang tinggal di tundra Lovozero. Menurut legenda, ia merampok dan membunuh orang Sami pada zaman dahulu. Orang Sami memanggil dewa-dewa mereka, yang murka lalu menyambarnya dengan petir dari air danau; Kuyva berubah menjadi batu keramat (seid) dengan ukuran raksasanya.",
      "en": "In Sami mythology Kuyva is a legendary giant who lived in the Lovozero tundra. According to legend he robbed and killed the Sami in ancient times.       "en": "The mark of his charred body is said to remain as a 74 m human silhouette on a cliff by Lake Seydozero. Studies show the figure formed naturally from moss, meltwater runoff and lichen. In another legend Kuyva is the leader of the Chud, enemies of the Sami from the south.",

      "id": "Mengapa bentuk alam yang unik sering dijelaskan lewat legenda raksasa?",
      "en": "Why are unusual natural forms often explained by giant legends?"
    }
  ],
  "images": [],
  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "kuyva-s1",
      "url": "https://ru.wikipedia.org/wiki/%D0%9A%D1%83%D0%B9%D0%B2%D0%B0",
      "title": "Куйва",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (ru)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "ru",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "kuyva-c01",
      "source_id": "kuyva-s1",
      "quote": "— персонаж мифологии саамов, легендарный великан, обитающий в Ловозерской тундре",
      "locator": "вступление",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuyva adalah tokoh mitologi Sami, raksasa legenda yang tinggal di tundra Lovozero.",
        "en": "Kuyva is a figure of Sami mythology, a legendary giant living in the Lovozero tundra."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuyva-c02",
      "source_id": "kuyva-s1",
      "quote": "Согласно легендам саамов, Куйва жил в древние времена в Ловозерских тундрах. Великан грабил и убивал саамов, за что был уничтожен саамскими языческими богами — «сожжён молниями, которые ударили из вод озера».",
      "locator": "вступление",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda Sami, Kuyva hidup pada zaman dahulu di tundra Lovozero; raksasa itu merampok dan membunuh orang Sami, sehingga dibinasakan para dewa pagan Sami, \"dibakar petir yang menyambar dari air danau\".",
        "en": "According to Sami legends Kuyva lived in ancient times in the Lovozero tundras; the giant robbed and killed the Sami and was destroyed by the Sami pagan gods, \"burned by lightning that struck from the waters of the lake\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuyva-c03",
      "source_id": "kuyva-s1",
      "quote": "После сожжения на скале у Сейдозера остался отпечаток его сожжённого тела, напоминающий человеческий силуэт. Высота силуэта составляет 74 м.",
      "locator": "вступление",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah dibakar, pada tebing di dekat Danau Seydozero tertinggal jejak tubuhnya yang hangus berbentuk siluet manusia setinggi 74 m.",
        "en": "After the burning, an imprint of his charred body resembling a human silhouette remained on a cliff by Lake Seydozero; the silhouette is 74 m high."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuyva-c04",
      "source_id": "kuyva-s1",
      "quote": "указывающие на вполне естественное происхождение следа на скале: «фигура образована мхом, мокрыми следами стока талых вод, лишайником и другими мелкими растениями»",
      "locator": "вступление",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kajian menunjukkan asal-usul jejak di tebing itu sepenuhnya alami: \"sosok itu terbentuk dari lumut, bekas basah aliran air lelehan salju, liken, dan tumbuhan kecil lainnya\".",
        "en": "Studies point to an entirely natural origin of the mark on the cliff: \"the figure is formed by moss, wet traces of meltwater runoff, lichen and other small plants\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuyva-c05",
      "source_id": "kuyva-s1",
      "quote": "Тогда саамы призвали своих богов, те увидели последствия учинённого Куйвой погрома, разгневались и поразили его молниями. Великан превратился в скалу, стал сейдом, сохранив свои исполинские размеры.",
      "locator": "вступление",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang Sami memanggil dewa-dewa mereka, yang murka melihat akibat amukan Kuyva dan menyambarnya dengan petir; raksasa itu menjadi batu, menjadi seid (batu keramat), dengan ukuran raksasanya tetap utuh.",
        "en": "The Sami called on their gods, who, seeing the havoc Kuyva had wrought, grew angry and struck him with lightning; the giant turned to rock and became a seid (sacred stone), keeping his gigantic size."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kuyva-c06",
      "source_id": "kuyva-s1",
      "quote": "Есть ещё одна легенда, где Куйва уже не тролль, а предводитель чудинов, врагов саамов, приходивших с юга и грабивших лопарей.",
      "locator": "Куйва в других легендах",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda lain, Kuyva bukan troll melainkan pemimpin orang Chud, musuh orang Sami yang datang dari selatan dan merampok mereka.",
        "en": "In another legend Kuyva is not a troll but the leader of the Chud, enemies of the Sami who came from the south and plundered them."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Siapa Kuyva",
        "en": "Who Kuyva was"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Raksasa yang dihukum petir para dewa.",
            "en": "A giant punished by the gods' lightning."
          },
          "claim_ids": [

            "kuyva-c05"
          ]
        },
        {

            "en": "The leader of the Chud, enemies of the Sami."
          },
          "claim_ids": [

          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ],

    "id": "Lännetär adalah tokoh dalam puisi mantra rakyat Finlandia, perempuan dari arah Barat, yang dicatat dalam kumpulan mantra suntingan Elias Lönnrot.",
    "en": "Lännetär is a figure in Finnish folk charm poetry, a woman of the West, recorded in Elias Lönnrot's collection of charms.",
    "claim_ids": [

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Lännetär adalah tokoh dalam puisi mantra yang tercatat dalam kumpulan \"Suomen kansan muinaisia loitsurunoja\" suntingan Elias Lönnrot; variannya menyebut bentuk rakyat Lemmetär. Dalam puisi mantra, \"putri-putri Barat\" sejajar dengan \"putra-putra Utara\", dan \"Istri Barat\" menjadi julukan bagi Nyonya Pohjola. Kutukan dianggap datang dari Utara dan pertolongan Tuhan dari Timur; Selatan selalu baik, sedangkan Barat condong ke dua arah.",
      "en": "Lännetär is a figure of charm poetry recorded in Elias Lönnrot's collection \"Suomen kansan muinaisia loitsurunoja\"; a variant gives the folk form Lemmetär. In charm poetry \"daughters of the West\" parallel the \"sons of the North\", and \"Wife of the West\" is an epithet of the Mistress of Pohjola. Curses were thought to come from the North and God's help from the East; the South is always good, while the West leans both ways.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lannetar-c01",
        "lannetar-c02",
        "lannetar-c03",
        "lannetar-c04"
      ]

  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Dalam puisi mantra Finlandia, arah mata angin bermakna moral: kutukan dari Utara, pertolongan Tuhan dari Timur, Selatan selalu baik, dan Barat, asal Lännetär, bersifat mendua.",
    "en": "In Finnish charm poetry the cardinal directions carry moral meaning: curses from the North, God's help from the East, the South always good, and the West, home of Lännetär, ambivalent.",

      "source_id": "lannetar-s1",
      "quote": "\"Lännen tyttäret\" kuitenkin tavataan \"Pohjoisen poikien\" kertosanana, samoin \"Lännen vaimo\" \"Pohjolan emännän\" mainesanana.",

      "source_id": "lannetar-s1",
      "quote": "Kirouksen käsitettiin tulevan Pohjoisesta ja Jumalan avun idästä. Etelä on aina hyvällä puolella, kun taas länsi näkyy kallistuvan kahtaanne.",
      "locator": "johdanto",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kutukan dianggap datang dari Utara dan pertolongan Tuhan dari Timur; Selatan selalu di pihak baik, sedangkan Barat tampak condong ke dua arah.",
        "en": "Curses were thought to come from the North and God's help from the East; the South is always on the good side, while the West appears to lean both ways."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Liekkiö adalah makhluk dalam tradisi rakyat Finlandia yang ditafsirkan beragam: penguasa pohon dan rumput, hantu anak yang dibunuh, atau burung arwah yang menjerit di hutan.",
    "en": "Liekkiö is a being of Finnish folk tradition with varied interpretations: lord of trees and grass, ghost of a murdered child, or a ghost bird shrieking in the forest.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "liekkio-c01",
      "liekkio-c03",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Liekkiö adalah makhluk legenda dalam tradisi rakyat Finlandia yang sosoknya ditafsirkan dengan berbagai cara. Mikael Agricola menyebutnya pertama kali pada 1551 sebagai berhala orang Häme yang menguasai rumput, akar, dan pohon. Kristfrid Ganander pada 1789 menggambarkannya sebagai hantu yang lahir dari anak-anak yang ditelantarkan, berteriak di hutan, dan menjadi penguasa pohon, rumput, dan akar.",
      "en": "Liekkiö is a legendary being of Finnish folk tradition interpreted in many ways. Mikael Agricola first mentioned it in 1551 as an idol of the Häme people ruling grasses, roots and trees. Kristfrid Ganander in 1789 described it as a ghost born of abandoned children who shouted in the forests and was lord of trees, grass and roots.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "liekkio-c01",
        "liekkio-c02",
        "liekkio-c03",
        "liekkio-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam tradisi Satakunta yang lebih kemudian, liekkiö dianggap jiwa atau hantu anak yang dibunuh; biasanya tidak berbahaya, tetapi mengganggu pelancong dengan tangisan. Menurut anggapan umum ia adalah burung arwah yang gelisah di kala gelap, mengikuti pengembara, dan bisa menirukan ucapan manusia. Dalam beberapa konteks ia berwujud seperti nyala api, semacam api tipuan.",
      "en": "In later Satakunta tradition the liekkiö was held to be the soul or ghost of a murdered child, usually harmless but disturbing travellers with its crying. By common belief it was a restless ghost bird in the dark that followed wanderers and could echo human speech. In some contexts it was flame-like, a kind of will-o'-the-wisp.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "liekkio-c05",
        "liekkio-c06",
        "liekkio-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sebuah kisah dari Merikarvia menyebut makhluk itu berhenti menirukan orang ketika ditanya di mana ia saat Kristus disalib. Liekkiö kadang juga menolong, misalnya memperingatkan orang yang tidur di hutan tentang pohon yang akan tumbang. Namanya bahkan muncul dalam terjemahan Alkitab Finlandia tahun 1642 untuk Yesaya 34:14.",
      "en": "A tale from Merikarvia says the being stopped echoing people when asked where it was when Christ was crucified. The liekkiö could also help, for instance warning someone asleep in the forest of a falling tree. Its name even appears in the 1642 Finnish Bible translation of Isaiah 34:14.",
      "claim_ids": [

        "liekkio-c10"
      ]

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c01",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Liekkiö on suomalaisen kansanperinteen taruolento. Liekkiön hahmosta on erilaisia tulkintoja.",
      "locator": "johdanto",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Liekkiö adalah makhluk legenda dalam tradisi rakyat Finlandia, dan sosoknya ditafsirkan dengan berbagai cara.",
        "en": "Liekkiö is a legendary being of Finnish folk tradition, and there are various interpretations of the figure."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c02",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Ensimmäisen kerran Liekkiön mainitsee Mikael Agricola vuonna 1551 ilmestyneen Dauidin psalttarin alkupuheen jumalaluettelossa hämäläisten tuntemana epäjumalana",
      "locator": "Kirjalliset maininnat",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Liekkiö pertama kali disebut Mikael Agricola dalam daftar dewa pada kata pengantar Mazmur Daud yang terbit tahun 1551, sebagai berhala yang dikenal orang Häme.",
        "en": "Liekkiö is first mentioned by Mikael Agricola in the list of gods in the preface to the Psalter of David published in 1551, as an idol known to the Tavastians (Häme people)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c03",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Lieckiö Rohot jwret ja puudh / hallitsi ja sencaltaiset mwdh.",
      "locator": "Kirjalliset maininnat",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Baris Agricola berbunyi: \"Lieckiö menguasai rumput, akar, dan pohon, dan hal-hal semacam itu.\"",
        "en": "Agricola's line reads: \"Lieckiö ruled grasses, roots and trees, and such other things.\""
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c04",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Kristfrid Ganander mainitsee teoksessaan Mythologica Fennica (1789) Liekkiön olevan ... heitteille jätetyistä lapsista syntynyt kummittelija, joka huusi metsissä ja pelästytty kiljumisellaan matkustavaisia. Hän oli puitten, ruohon ja juurien herra.",
      "locator": "Kirjalliset maininnat",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Kristfrid Ganander (Mythologica Fennica, 1789), Liekkiö adalah hantu yang lahir dari anak-anak yang ditelantarkan, berteriak di hutan dan menakuti pelancong dengan jeritannya; ia penguasa pohon, rumput, dan akar.",
        "en": "According to Kristfrid Ganander (Mythologica Fennica, 1789), Liekkiö is a ghost born of abandoned children, who shouted in the forests and frightened travellers with its shrieks; it was lord of trees, grass and roots."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c05",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Myöhemmässä satakuntalaisessa perinteessä liekkiö miellettiin surmatun lapsen sieluksi tai kummitukseksi. Liekkiö ei tavallisesti tehnyt pahaa kenellekään, mutta saattoi häiritä matkustavaisia itkemällä ja ulisemalla.",
      "locator": "Kirjalliset maininnat",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam tradisi Satakunta yang lebih kemudian, liekkiö dipahami sebagai jiwa atau hantu anak yang dibunuh; biasanya ia tidak mencelakai siapa pun, tetapi bisa mengganggu pelancong dengan tangisan dan lolongan.",
        "en": "In later Satakunta tradition the liekkiö was understood as the soul or ghost of a murdered child; it usually harmed no one but could disturb travellers by crying and howling."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c06",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Jossain yhteydessä liekkiö oli liekinkaltainen, jolloin kyse oli eräänlaisesta virvatulesta.",
      "locator": "Kirjalliset maininnat",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam beberapa konteks liekkiö berwujud seperti nyala api, semacam api tipuan (will-o'-the-wisp).",
        "en": "In some contexts the liekkiö was flame-like, a kind of will-o'-the-wisp."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c07",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Merikarvialta 1911 kerätty kansantarina kertoo liekkiön kohtaamisesta ... Sitten se lakkasi kun kysyttiin: \"Missä sinä sillon olit kun Kristus oli ristin päällä?\"",
      "locator": "Kirjalliset maininnat",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kisah dari Merikarvia (1911), makhluk itu berhenti meniru suara orang ketika ditanya: \"Di mana kamu ketika Kristus di kayu salib?\"",
        "en": "In a tale from Merikarvia (1911), the being stopped echoing people when asked: \"Where were you when Christ was on the cross?\""
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c08",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Yleisen käsityksen mukaan liekkiö oli pimeän aikana levottomasti liikehtivä kummituslintu, joka seurasi vaeltavaa matkamiestä, ja eri tavoin äännellessään saattoi kirkua, huutaa tai jopa toistaa ihmisen puhetta.",
      "locator": "Kirjalliset maininnat",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut anggapan umum, liekkiö adalah burung hantu (burung arwah) yang bergerak gelisah di kala gelap, mengikuti pengembara, dan bisa menjerit, berteriak, bahkan menirukan ucapan manusia.",
        "en": "According to common belief the liekkiö was a ghost bird moving restlessly in the dark, following a wandering traveller, and could shriek, shout or even repeat human speech."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c09",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Kerrotaan, että liekkiö oli joskus varoittanut metsässä nukkuvaa kohta kaatuvasta puusta.",
      "locator": "Metsänhaltija, jumala, vai kummitteleva lapsivainaja",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon liekkiö pernah memperingatkan orang yang tidur di hutan tentang pohon yang akan tumbang.",
        "en": "It is said that the liekkiö once warned someone sleeping in the forest of a tree about to fall."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "liekkio-c10",
      "source_id": "liekkio-s1",
      "quote": "Vuoden 1642 Biblia kääntää Jesaja 34:14:n Edomin tuhoa käsittelevän kohdan seuraavasti ... \"Silloin pitä männingäiset ja köpelit cohtaman toinen toistans, ja yhden lieckiön pitä toista huutaman\"",
      "locator": "Vanhoissa raamatunkäännöksissä",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Alkitab Finlandia tahun 1642 menerjemahkan Yesaya 34:14 dengan menyebut \"lieckiö\" yang saling memanggil.",
        "en": "The Finnish Bible of 1642 renders Isaiah 34:14 with \"lieckiö\" calling to one another."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Ljubi atau lubia adalah demon air dan badai dalam mitologi Albania, naga betina berkepala banyak yang mengeringkan air kecuali diberi kurban perawan.",
    "en": "The ljubi or lubia is a water and storm demon of Albanian mythology, a many-headed female dragon who dries up the waters unless given a virgin sacrifice.",

    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi dan cerita rakyat Albania, lubia atau ljubi adalah demon air dan badai, biasanya digambarkan sebagai naga betina berwujud ular raksasa berkepala banyak yang mirip kulshedra. Dalam kepercayaan Albania Selatan ia adalah dewi badai, dan ia juga disebut \"ibu lubia\".",
      "en": "In Albanian mythology and folklore the lubia or ljubi is a water and storm demon, generally pictured as an enormous she-dragon in serpent form with many heads, much like the kulshedra. In Southern Albanian belief she is a storm deity, and she is also called \"mother lubia\".",

    },
    {
      "id": "Lubia dipercaya tinggal di kebun sayur yang indah. Ia bisa membuat air mengering kecuali seorang perawan dikurbankan kepadanya.       "en": "The kulshedra and the ljubi are very similar in appearance and function, and the kulshedra may be another name for the ljubi, one with more power and notoriety in Albanian culture.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "ljubi-c01",
      "source_id": "ljubi-s1",
      "quote": "The lubia or ljubi is a water and storm demon in Albanian mythology and folklore, usually depicted as a huge multi-headed female serpentine dragon similar to the kulshedra. In Southern Albanian beliefs, she is a storm deity.",
      "locator": "lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lubia atau ljubi adalah demon air dan badai dalam mitologi dan cerita rakyat Albania, biasanya digambarkan sebagai naga betina berwujud ular raksasa berkepala banyak yang mirip kulshedra; dalam kepercayaan Albania Selatan ia adalah dewi badai.",
        "en": "The lubia or ljubi is a water and storm demon of Albanian mythology and folklore, usually depicted as a huge multi-headed female serpentine dragon similar to the kulshedra; in Southern Albanian belief she is a storm deity."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ljubi-c02",
      "source_id": "ljubi-s1",
      "quote": "She is also referred to as ‘mother lubia’.",
      "locator": "lead",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga disebut \"ibu lubia\".",
        "en": "She is also called \"mother lubia\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ljubi-c03",
      "source_id": "ljubi-s1",
      "quote": "Lubia is depicted as a female demon. She is believed to live in a wonderful vegetable garden. Lubia, as a powerful demon, can cause the waters to dry up unless a virgin is sacrificed to her.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lubia digambarkan sebagai demon perempuan yang dipercaya tinggal di kebun sayur yang indah; sebagai demon yang kuat, ia bisa membuat air mengering kecuali seorang perawan dikurbankan kepadanya.",
        "en": "Lubia is depicted as a female demon believed to live in a wonderful vegetable garden; as a powerful demon she can make the waters dry up unless a virgin is sacrificed to her."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ljubi-c04",
      "source_id": "ljubi-s1",
      "quote": "Lubia is described as having multiple heads, anywhere from 7-100. Like the Lernaean Hydra, when one of her heads is cut off, another grows in its place. Lubia is also known to have irresistible taste for flesh, especially that of little girls.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lubia digambarkan berkepala banyak, antara 7 hingga 100; seperti Hydra Lerna, bila satu kepalanya dipenggal, kepala lain tumbuh menggantikannya, dan ia sangat menggemari daging, terutama daging gadis kecil.",
        "en": "Lubia is described as having many heads, anywhere from 7 to 100; like the Lernaean Hydra, when one head is cut off another grows in its place, and she has an irresistible taste for flesh, especially that of little girls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ljubi-c05",
      "source_id": "ljubi-s1",
      "quote": "The Kulshedra and Ljubi are very similar in both appearance and function in Albanian mythology.",
      "locator": "Comparison",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Albania, kulshedra dan ljubi sangat mirip dalam wujud maupun fungsi.",
        "en": "In Albanian mythology the kulshedra and the ljubi are very similar in both appearance and function."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ljubi-c06",
      "source_id": "ljubi-s1",
      "quote": "This matches the description given to Ljubi, and may be another name for it, albeit one with more power and notoriety within Albanian culture.",
      "locator": "Comparison",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gambaran kulshedra cocok dengan gambaran ljubi, dan kulshedra mungkin nama lain untuknya, meski lebih kuat dan lebih terkenal dalam budaya Albania.",
        "en": "The kulshedra's description matches that of the ljubi and may be another name for it, though one with more power and notoriety in Albanian culture."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Naga Lukoml adalah naga dalam legenda Belarus yang tinggal di dalam batu besar dekat Hohaleuka, menjahitkan pakaian bagi penduduk, dan kematiannya oleh petir memicu hujan dahsyat.",
    "en": "The Lukoml dragon is a dragon from a legend of Belarus who lived in a boulder near Hohaleuka, sewed clothes for villagers, and whose death by lightning brought terrible rains.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "lukomlskoye-dragon-c01",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Naga Lukoml adalah makhluk mitos yang dalam legenda dikaitkan dengan sebuah batu besar dekat desa Hohaleuka, distrik Chashniki. Batu itu konon dulu berlubang seperti jendela dan naga tinggal di dalamnya. Orang membawa sedikit uang ke batu itu, meminta naga menjahitkan pakaian, dan keesokan harinya mengambil pakaian yang sudah jadi. Dari luar ia tampak seperti manusia, tetapi kadang berwujud pelangi yang mengisap air Danau Lukoml ke langit.",
      "en": "The Lukoml dragon is a mythical monster that legend links with a boulder near the village of Hohaleuka in Chashniki district. The stone was said to have a window-like hole with the dragon living inside. People brought a little money, asked the dragon to sew clothes, and collected the finished garments the next day. Outwardly it looked like a man, but sometimes it became a rainbow sucking the water of Lake Lukoml up into the sky.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lukomlskoye-dragon-c01",
        "lukomlskoye-dragon-c02",
        "lukomlskoye-dragon-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Naga itu takut petir dan meminta seorang gadis membangunkannya bila badai datang, tetapi gadis itu sengaja tidak membangunkannya. Naga terbangun, menampar wajah si gadis hingga berbekas selamanya, lalu tewas disambar petir. Hujan dahsyat pun turun. Orang menguburkan naga itu, tetapi angin mengangkat jasadnya, dan hujan baru berhenti setelah naga dikubur lagi dengan tanah yang dibawa anak-anak kecil dalam kantong.",
      "en": "The dragon feared lightning and asked a girl to wake it if a storm came, but she deliberately did not. The dragon woke, slapped her face, leaving a lasting mark, and was then killed by lightning. A terrible downpour followed. People buried the dragon, but the wind lifted the body out, and the rain stopped only after it was buried again with earth carried by small children in little bags.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lukomlskoye-dragon-c04",

        "lukomlskoye-dragon-c06",

    {
      "id": "Dalam legenda ini naga tampil sebagai penjaga keseimbangan antara air bumi dan air langit, sehingga kematiannya menimbulkan bencana. Hal ini menunjukkan kaitannya dengan dewa Veles, yang mungkin menjadi saingan dewa guntur Perun. Batu dalam legenda masih ada dengan nama Zmeyeu kamen, salah satu batu besar terbesar di Belarus dengan panjang 9,8 m dan tinggi 2,4 m.",
      "en": "In this legend the dragon is a keeper of the balance between earthly and heavenly water, so its death causes disaster. This points to a link with the god Veles, who may have been a rival of the thunder god Perun.   ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c01",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "Лукомскі цмок — міфічная пачвара, якую паданні звязваюць з валуном ля вёскі Гогалеўка Чашніцкага раёна.",
      "locator": "уводзіны",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Naga Lukoml (Lukomski tsmok) adalah makhluk mitos yang dalam legenda dikaitkan dengan sebuah batu besar dekat desa Hohaleuka, distrik Chashniki.",
        "en": "The Lukoml dragon (Lukomski tsmok) is a mythical monster that legends link with a boulder near the village of Hohaleuka in Chashniki district."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c02",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "Камень раней быццам меў адтуліну-акно, і цмок жыў унутры. Людзі прыносілі да валуна невялічкія грошы і прасілі цмока пашыць адзенне. А на другі дзень ужо забіралі гатовую вопратку.",
      "locator": "Паданне",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon batu itu dulu berlubang seperti jendela dan sang naga tinggal di dalamnya; orang membawa sedikit uang ke batu itu dan meminta naga menjahitkan pakaian, dan keesokan harinya mereka mengambil pakaian yang sudah jadi.",
        "en": "The stone was said to have once had a window-like hole, and the dragon lived inside; people brought small sums of money to the boulder and asked the dragon to sew clothes, and the next day they collected the finished garments."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c03",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "Звонку цмок выглядаў як чалавек, але часам прымаў выгляд вясёлкі і падымаўся над Лукомскім возерам, смактаў з яго ваду і перапампоўваў на неба.",
      "locator": "Паданне",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dari luar naga itu tampak seperti manusia, tetapi kadang berwujud pelangi, naik di atas Danau Lukoml, mengisap airnya, dan memompanya ke langit.",
        "en": "Outwardly the dragon looked like a man, but sometimes it took the form of a rainbow, rose over Lake Lukoml, sucked up its water and pumped it into the sky."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c04",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "Цмок асцерагаўся маланак. І аднойчы, перад тым як заснуць на полі, ён папярэдзіў дзяўчыну, каб тая пабудзіла яго, калі пачнецца навальніца. Сабраліся хмары, зазіхацелі маланкі, але дзяўчына не будзіла цмока.",
      "locator": "Паданне",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Naga itu takut petir; suatu kali sebelum tidur di ladang ia meminta seorang gadis membangunkannya bila badai datang, tetapi ketika awan berkumpul dan petir menyambar, gadis itu tidak membangunkannya.",
        "en": "The dragon feared lightning; once, before sleeping in a field, it asked a girl to wake it if a storm began, but when the clouds gathered and lightning flashed the girl did not wake it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c05",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "Раптам ён сам прачнуўся і, зразумеўшы здраду, пляснуў дзяўчыну далонню па твары, з-за чаго ў яе назаўсёды застаўся след. У наступнае імгненне цмока забіла маланка.",
      "locator": "Паданне",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tiba-tiba ia terbangun sendiri, menyadari pengkhianatan itu, dan menampar wajah si gadis sehingga bekasnya tertinggal selamanya; sesaat kemudian petir membunuh naga itu.",
        "en": "Suddenly it woke by itself, realised the betrayal and slapped the girl's face, leaving a mark forever; the next moment lightning killed the dragon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c06",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "Людзі даведаліся, што трэба хутчэй закапаць цмока — і бедствы скончацца. Так і зрабілі. Але падняўся вецер, які выдзьмуў цела забітага з-пад зямлі.",
      "locator": "Паданне",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lalu turun hujan dahsyat; orang tahu bahwa naga itu harus segera dikuburkan agar bencana berakhir, dan mereka melakukannya, tetapi angin bertiup dan mengangkat jasadnya dari dalam tanah.",
        "en": "A terrible downpour followed; people learned that the dragon had to be buried quickly to end the calamity, and they did so, but a wind rose and blew the body out of the ground."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c07",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "І толькі тады людзі ўспомнілі, што трэба, каб зямлю насілі маленькія дзеці ў торбачках. Так цмока пахавалі ў другі раз, і залёвы нарэшце спыніліся.",
      "locator": "Паданне",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Barulah orang ingat bahwa tanahnya harus dibawa oleh anak-anak kecil dalam kantong; maka naga itu dikuburkan untuk kedua kalinya, dan hujan deras akhirnya berhenti.",
        "en": "Only then did people remember that the earth had to be carried by little children in small bags; so the dragon was buried a second time, and the downpours finally stopped."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c08",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "У паданні цмок паўстае адным з даглядчыкам прыроднага балансу, размеркавальнікам раўнавагі паміж зямной вадой і нябеснай. Таму яго смерць выклікае прыродную катастрофу.",
      "locator": "Інтэрпрэтацыя",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda ini naga tampil sebagai salah satu penjaga keseimbangan alam, pengatur keseimbangan antara air bumi dan air langit; karena itu kematiannya menimbulkan bencana alam.",
        "en": "In the legend the dragon appears as a keeper of natural balance, regulating the equilibrium between earthly and heavenly water; hence its death causes a natural catastrophe."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c09",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "Гэта паказвае на сувязь персанажа з вобразам язычніцкага бога Вялеса, які мог быць супернікам грамабоя Пяруна.",
      "locator": "Інтэрпрэтацыя",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hal ini menunjukkan kaitan tokoh ini dengan dewa pagan Veles, yang mungkin menjadi saingan dewa guntur Perun.",
        "en": "This points to a link between the figure and the pagan god Veles, who may have been a rival of the thunder god Perun."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-c10",
      "source_id": "lukomlskoye-dragon-s1",
      "quote": "Валун, аб якім гаворыцца ў паданні, існуе і ў нашыя дні і вядомы як Змееў камень. Гэта адзін з самых вялікіх валуноў у Беларусі: яго даўжыня — 9,8 м, вышыня — 2,4 м.",
      "locator": "Цікавыя факты",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Batu besar dalam legenda itu masih ada dan dikenal sebagai Zmeyeu kamen (Batu Ular), salah satu batu besar terbesar di Belarus, panjangnya 9,8 m dan tingginya 2,4 m.",
        "en": "The boulder in the legend still exists and is known as Zmeyeu kamen (Serpent Stone), one of the largest boulders in Belarus: 9.8 m long and 2.4 m high."
      }
    }
  ],

      "quote": "Згідно найпоширенішої, цей демон з'являється вночі на узбіччях доріг в червоних відблисках багаття. Іноді назву сутності пояснюють тим, що у неї «сиром'ясне обличчя», тобто не вкрите шкірою.",

      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {

      "id": "manush-lolo-c04",
      "source_id": "manush-lolo-s1",

      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {

      "source_id": "manush-lolo-s1",
      "quote": "Серед угорських циган чанґо популярна легенда про те, що саме цей демон подарував їм скрипку. Він виготовив інструмент з членів родини Маріки, натомість допоміг їй причарувати вродливого мисливця, а потім забрав дівчину і її коханого до пекла.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Miernik adalah demon ladang dalam kepercayaan rakyat: jiwa juru ukur tanah yang tidak jujur, muncul sebagai nyala api pada malam hari di tempat-tempat yang pernah diukur.",
    "en": "The miernik is a field demon of folk belief: the soul of a dishonest land surveyor, appearing as a flame at night where land was once measured.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "miernik-c01",
      "miernik-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Miernik atau mierniczy adalah demon ladang dari golongan api tipuan, yakni jiwa juru ukur tanah yang tidak jujur yang sedang menebus dosa. Menurut kepercayaan rakyat, jiwa para juru ukur yang salah mengukur ladang muncul di padang rumput, lapangan, dan jalan tempat pengukuran pernah dilakukan, setelah senja dan pada malam hari, terutama pada masa Prapaskah dan Adven.",
      "en": "The miernik or mierniczy is a field demon of the will-o'-the-wisp kind, the penitent soul of a dishonest land surveyor. In folk belief the souls of surveyors who measured fields wrongly appeared on meadows, squares and roads where measuring had taken place, after dusk and at night, mainly during Lent and Advent.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "miernik-c01",
        "miernik-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mereka biasanya tampak sebagai nyala api kecil yang merayap atau terbang, tetapi kadang berwujud tangan berapi yang memegang pena, tiang api raksasa, atau sosok yang membawa lilin dan alat ukur. Mereka membenci siulan dan menyerang orang yang bersiul, meski siulan kadang justru mengusir mereka. Di Silesia, miernik juga nama untuk cahaya rawa.",
      "en": "They usually appeared as a creeping or flying flame, but sometimes as a fiery hand holding a pen, huge pillars of fire, or a figure carrying a candle and surveying tools. They hated whistling and attacked those who whistled, though whistling could sometimes drive them away. In Silesia, miernik is also a name for the marsh light.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "miernik-c01",
        "miernik-c03",
        "miernik-c04",
        "miernik-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Kepercayaan ini menghukum ketidakjujuran dalam mengukur tanah: jiwa juru ukur curang harus menebus dosanya di tempat-tempat yang ia ukur.",
    "en": "The belief punishes dishonesty in land measurement: a cheating surveyor's soul must do penance where he measured.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "miernik-c01",
      "source_id": "miernik-s1",
      "quote": "Miernik, mierniczy – polny demon z kategorii błędnych ogni; pokutująca dusza niesprawiedliwego geometry, wyobrażana często jako pełzający lub latający płomyk.",
      "locator": "wstęp",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Miernik atau mierniczy adalah demon ladang dari golongan api tipuan: jiwa juru ukur tanah yang tidak jujur yang sedang menebus dosa, sering dibayangkan sebagai nyala api kecil yang merayap atau terbang.",
        "en": "The miernik or mierniczy is a field demon of the will-o'-the-wisp kind: the penitent soul of a dishonest land surveyor, often pictured as a creeping or flying flame."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "miernik-c02",
      "source_id": "miernik-s1",
      "quote": "Według wierzeń ludowych dusze mierniczych, którzy źle wymierzyli pola, pojawiały się wszędzie tam, gdzie dokonywano pomiarów, tj. na łąkach, placach i drogach. Pokazywały się po zapadnięciu zmroku oraz w nocy. Występowały głównie w okresie wielkopostnym i adwentu.",
      "locator": "wstęp",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kepercayaan rakyat, jiwa para juru ukur yang salah mengukur ladang muncul di mana pun pengukuran pernah dilakukan, yakni di padang rumput, lapangan, dan jalan; mereka tampak setelah senja dan pada malam hari, terutama pada masa Prapaskah dan Adven.",
        "en": "In folk belief the souls of surveyors who measured fields wrongly appeared wherever measuring had been done, on meadows, squares and roads; they showed themselves after dusk and at night, mainly during Lent and Advent."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "miernik-c03",
      "source_id": "miernik-s1",
      "quote": "Niekiedy przybierały kształt ognistej ręki trzymającej pióro, światełek albo ogromnych słupów ognia, czasami postaci trzymającej zapaloną świecę i przyrządy geodezyjne.",
      "locator": "wstęp",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang mereka berwujud tangan berapi yang memegang pena, cahaya-cahaya kecil, atau tiang api raksasa, kadang sosok yang memegang lilin menyala dan alat ukur tanah.",
        "en": "Sometimes they took the form of a fiery hand holding a pen, small lights or huge pillars of fire, sometimes a figure holding a lit candle and surveying instruments."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "miernik-c04",
      "source_id": "miernik-s1",
      "quote": "Nienawidziły gwizdania, biły i deptały śmiałków, którzy ośmielili się zagwizdać w ich obecności, ale niekiedy właśnie gwizdaniem można było miernika odstraszyć.",
      "locator": "wstęp",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka membenci siulan dan memukul serta menginjak-injak orang yang berani bersiul di dekat mereka, tetapi kadang justru siulan bisa mengusir miernik.",
        "en": "They hated whistling and beat and trampled those who dared whistle in their presence, though sometimes whistling was exactly what scared a miernik away."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "miernik-c05",
      "source_id": "miernik-s1",
      "quote": "miernik – występująca na Śląsku nazwa świetlika bagiennego",
      "locator": "Inne znaczenia",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Silesia, miernik juga nama untuk cahaya rawa (świetlik bagienny).",
        "en": "In Silesia, miernik is also a name for the marsh light (świetlik bagienny)."
      }
    }
  ],

      "id": "Mirigy, yang juga disebut merigy, mirvigy, atau mörvigy, adalah nama penyakit dalam pengobatan rakyat Hungaria dan sekaligus demon penyakit dalam kepercayaan asli Hungaria.       "en": "Mirigy, also called merigy, mirvigy or mörvigy, is a disease name in Hungarian folk medicine and also a disease demon of the ancient Hungarian religion. It was held to cause bubonic plague and other illnesses with swollen glands, some skin diseases, and severe, rapid internal diseases.",

      "en": "Most records of it since the mid-19th century come from the eastern half of the country, mainly Székelyföld, and it is very probably identical with the disease demon csuma. The poet Mihály Vörösmarty used the figure in the drama Csongor és Tünde.",

      "source_id": "mirigy-s1",
      "quote": "A mirigy (más néven merigy, mirvigy, mörvigy, mirigyes dögletesség, mirigyhalál, mirigy-kór) a magyar népi gyógyászat betegségelnevezése és a magyar ősvallás egyik betegségdémona.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",

        "en": "Mirigy (also merigy, mirvigy, mörvigy, mirigyes dögletesség, mirigyhalál, mirigy-kór) is a disease name in Hungarian folk medicine and one of the disease demons of the ancient Hungarian religion."

    {
      "id": "mirigy-c02",
      "source_id": "mirigy-s1",
      "quote": "A 19. század közepe óta feljegyzett adatok zöme az ország keleti feléből, főként Székelyföldről való, hasonlóan a csumához, mellyel nagy valószínűséggel azonos.",
      "locator": "bevezető",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {

      "id": "mirigy-c03",
      "source_id": "mirigy-s1",
      "quote": "A mirigy a bubópestis, más nyirokcsomó-duzzanattal járó betegségek, néhány bőrbetegség, a hullafoltok, a súlyos és gyors lefolyású belső betegségek, a pestises gyulladás, és a pestises szepszis okozója, betegségdémona.",
      "locator": "bevezető",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mirigy adalah penyebab dan demon penyakit bagi pes bubo, penyakit lain dengan pembengkakan kelenjar getah bening, beberapa penyakit kulit, lebam mayat, penyakit dalam yang berat dan cepat, radang pes, dan sepsis pes.",
        "en": "Mirigy is the cause and disease demon (betegségdémona) of bubonic plague (bubópestis), other illnesses with swollen lymph nodes, some skin diseases, livor mortis (hullafoltok), severe and rapid internal diseases, plague inflammation and plague sepsis (pestises szepszis)."

    "en": "Nežit is a disease demon of Slavic mythology that settles in a person's head and causes various physical illnesses.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "nezit-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Nežit adalah demon dari mitologi Slavia yang menimbulkan berbagai penyakit fisik pada manusia. Ia bersarang di kepala, mengeringkan otak, memelintir mulut, merusak gigi, membengkokkan leher, menulikan telinga, dan membutakan mata.",
      "en": "Nežit is a demon of Slavic mythology that causes various physical illnesses. It settles in the head, dries the brain, twists the mouth, wears down the teeth, bends the neck, deafens the ears and blinds the eyes.",

        "nezit-c01"
      ]
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Luupainaja adalah roh dalam mitologi Estonia yang menindih orang dan hewan yang sedang tidur pada malam hari, dan bisa diusir dengan menggerakkan ibu jari kaki atau menyalakan api.",
    "en": "The luupainaja is a spirit of Estonian mythology that presses on sleeping people and animals at night and can be driven off by moving the big toe or lighting a fire.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "nightmare-c01",
      "nightmare-c03",
      "nightmare-c06",
      "nightmare-c07"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Estonia, luupainaja, yang juga disebut painaja, painak, atau tallaja, adalah roh yang menindih orang yang sedang tidur. Ia datang pada malam hari dan menekan tubuh korban sehingga korban tidak bisa bergerak. Orang tua dahulu percaya tindihan yang lama bisa membawa kematian, dan luupainaja juga menindih hewan pekerja untuk melemahkan tenaganya.",
      "en": "In Estonian mythology the luupainaja, also called painaja, painak or tallaja, is a spirit that presses down on sleepers. It comes at night and weighs on the victim's body so that they cannot move. The old folk believed prolonged pressing could kill, and the luupainaja also pressed on working animals to sap their strength.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nightmare-c01",
        "nightmare-c02",
        "nightmare-c03",
        "nightmare-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam kebanyakan cerita ia tampak sebagai perempuan muda, tetapi juga sebagai perempuan atau lelaki tua, atau sebagai kucing hitam, kuda, dan anjing. Untuk melepaskan diri, orang menggerakkan ibu jari kaki atau menyalakan api, karena luupainaja takut cahaya.",
      "en": "In most tales it appears as a young woman, but also as an old woman or old man, or as a black cat, horse or dog. To get free, people moved their big toe or lit a fire, since the luupainaja fears light.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nightmare-c05",
        "nightmare-c06",
        "nightmare-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "M. J. Eisen mencatat bahwa luupainaja memilih korban dan menindihnya setiap malam seperti batu di dada. Menurut kisah yang dikenal luas, bila lubang masuknya ditutup dan api dinyalakan, ia tampak sebagai gadis muda yang kemudian dinikahi korbannya, sampai suaminya mencabut sumbat lubang itu dan sang istri lenyap. Eisen menyimpulkan bahwa luupainaja sebenarnya penyihir, manusia yang jiwanya meninggalkan tubuh dan mengambil berbagai wujud.",
      "en": "M. J. Eisen recorded that the luupainaja chooses a victim and presses on them nightly like a stone on the chest. In a widely known tale, when its entry hole is stopped and a light is struck it appears as a young girl whom the victim marries, until the husband pulls the plug from the hole and the wife vanishes. Eisen concluded that the luupainaja is really a witch, a human whose soul leaves the body and takes various forms.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c01",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s1",
      "quote": "Luupainaja on eesti mütoloogias magajaid painav vaim.",
      "locator": "sissejuhatus",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Estonia, luupainaja adalah roh yang menindih orang yang sedang tidur.",
        "en": "In Estonian mythology the luupainaja is a spirit that presses down on sleepers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c02",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s1",
      "quote": "Luupainaja teiste nimetuste hulka kuuluvad painaja, painak, panijas, paanjas, painakane, luupaanija, luupaine, luupatak, tallaja.",
      "locator": "sissejuhatus",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama lain luupainaja antara lain painaja, painak, panijas, paanjas, painakane, luupaanija, luupaine, luupatak, dan tallaja.",
        "en": "Other names of the luupainaja include painaja, painak, panijas, paanjas, painakane, luupaanija, luupaine, luupatak and tallaja."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c03",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s1",
      "quote": "Nimelt käivad tallajad öösiti oma ohvrit painamas. Nad rõhuvad oma ohvri keha, nii et too liigutada ei saa.",
      "locator": "sissejuhatus",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tallaja datang pada malam hari menindih korbannya, menekan tubuh korban sehingga ia tidak bisa bergerak.",
        "en": "The tallajad come at night to press on their victim, weighing down the body so the victim cannot move."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c04",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s1",
      "quote": "Vanarahvas arvas, et pikaaegne painamine võib surma tuua. Tallajad ei käi ainult inimesi piinamas, vaid üritavad ka tööloomade jõudu oma rõhumisega nõrgendada.",
      "locator": "sissejuhatus",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang tua dahulu percaya bahwa tindihan yang berlangsung lama bisa membawa kematian; tallaja tidak hanya menyiksa manusia tetapi juga berusaha melemahkan tenaga hewan pekerja dengan tindihannya.",
        "en": "The old folk believed prolonged pressing could bring death; the tallajad torment not only people but also try to weaken working animals by pressing on them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c05",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s1",
      "quote": "Enamikus juttudes on öeldud, et tallajad ilmutavad end noore naisena, kuid ei pelga ära ka vana naise ja vana mehena ilmumist. Samuti on loomadega – luupainajaid kirjeldatakse nii mustade kassidena, hobustena, koertena jne.",
      "locator": "sissejuhatus",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kebanyakan cerita, tallaja menampakkan diri sebagai perempuan muda, tetapi juga sebagai perempuan tua atau lelaki tua; luupainaja juga digambarkan sebagai kucing hitam, kuda, anjing, dan sebagainya.",
        "en": "In most tales the tallajad appear as a young woman, though also as an old woman or old man; luupainajad are also described as black cats, horses, dogs and so on."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c06",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s1",
      "quote": "Lihtsaim neist on suure varba liigutamine. Nimelt arvati, et kui tunned luupainajat end rõhuvat, tuleb suurt varvast liigutada ning siis tallaja põgeneb.",
      "locator": "sissejuhatus",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cara paling mudah untuk melepaskan diri adalah menggerakkan ibu jari kaki: orang percaya bila merasa ditindih luupainaja, ibu jari kaki harus digerakkan, maka tallaja akan lari.",
        "en": "The simplest way to get free is to move the big toe: it was believed that if you feel the luupainaja pressing on you, you must move your big toe and the tallaja will flee."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c07",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s1",
      "quote": "Teiseks levinud võimaluseks oli tule põlema panemine, valgust pidavat luupainajad kartma.",
      "locator": "sissejuhatus",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cara umum lainnya adalah menyalakan api, karena luupainaja konon takut cahaya.",
        "en": "Another common way was to light a fire, as the luupainajad were said to fear light."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c08",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s2",
      "quote": "Luupainaja valib enesele mingisuguse isiku ohvriks ja hakkab seda kas igal ööl ehk mõne öö takka painamas käima, nagu kivi rinnul ehk kehal lasudes",
      "locator": "Eesti mütoloogia",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut M. J. Eisen, luupainaja memilih seseorang sebagai korban dan datang menindihnya setiap malam atau selang beberapa malam, seperti batu yang menimpa dada atau tubuh.",
        "en": "According to M. J. Eisen, the luupainaja chooses a person as its victim and comes to press on them every night or every few nights, lying like a stone on the chest or body."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c09",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s2",
      "quote": "Laialt tuntakse lugu, mille järele sissetuleku-augu sulgumise ja tulesüütamise peale luupainaja ilmub noorena tütarlapsena, kelle painatav hiljemini enesele kosib.",
      "locator": "Eesti mütoloogia",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dikenal luas kisah bahwa bila lubang masuknya ditutup dan api dinyalakan, luupainaja menampakkan diri sebagai gadis muda, yang kemudian dilamar oleh orang yang ditindihnya.",
        "en": "A widely known tale says that when its entry hole is closed and a light is struck, the luupainaja appears as a young girl, whom the person she pressed later marries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c10",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s2",
      "quote": "Mõlemad elavad õnnelikult, kuni abielumees 7 ehk 14 aastat pärast pulmi luupainaja sissetuleku-augu eest pulga võtab, ja kohe kaob naine nagu õhk toast.",
      "locator": "Eesti mütoloogia",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Keduanya hidup bahagia sampai sang suami, 7 atau 14 tahun setelah pernikahan, mencabut sumbat dari lubang masuk luupainaja, dan seketika sang istri lenyap dari ruangan seperti udara.",
        "en": "Both live happily until the husband, 7 or 14 years after the wedding, pulls the plug from the luupainaja's entry hole, and at once the wife vanishes from the room like air."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c11",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s2",
      "quote": "Nagu eelmistest seletustest selgub, on luupainaja inimene, kuid mitte tavaline inimene, vaid niisugune, kes enesele iseäraliku tarkusega vaimude omaduse pärinud, teise sõnaga nõid.",
      "locator": "Eesti mütoloogia",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Eisen menyimpulkan bahwa luupainaja adalah manusia, tetapi bukan manusia biasa, melainkan orang yang dengan pengetahuan khusus memperoleh sifat roh, dengan kata lain seorang penyihir.",
        "en": "Eisen concludes that the luupainaja is a human, but not an ordinary one: someone who by special knowledge has acquired the qualities of spirits, in other words a witch."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nightmare-c12",
      "source_id": "nightmare-s2",
      "quote": "Tõepoolest ei muuda luupainaja inimlik keha ennast sugugi, vaid hing lahkub kehast, enesele lahkudes üht ehk teist kuju võttes.",
      "locator": "Eesti mütoloogia",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tubuh manusia luupainaja sebenarnya tidak berubah; jiwanya yang meninggalkan tubuh dan mengambil satu atau lain wujud.",
        "en": "The luupainaja's human body does not really change; rather the soul leaves the body, taking one form or another as it goes."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Oblakinia adalah demon Slavia dari tradisi Serbia-Kroasia, sejenis vila bertubuh kecil dan berkulit biru yang menjahit gaun dari salju, embun, dan awan.",
    "en": "The oblakinia is a Serbo-Croatian Slavic demon, a small blue-skinned kind of vila who sews gowns from snow, dew and clouds.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Oblakinia atau obłoczyca adalah demon Slavia dari tradisi Serbia-Kroasia, sejenis vila. Ia bertubuh kecil, setinggi separuh orang dewasa, ramping, dan bergerak ringan melayang di udara. Wajahnya tirus, rambutnya panjang berwarna biru langit, matanya besar, dan kulitnya biru. Pekerjaan utamanya menjahit gaun dari kepingan salju, tetes embun, serpihan awan, kabut, dan benang laba-laba.",
      "en": "The oblakinia or obłoczyca is a Serbo-Croatian Slavic demon, a kind of vila. She is small, half the height of an adult, slender, and floats lightly through the air. Her face is thin, her long hair sky-blue, her eyes large and her skin blue. Her main occupation is sewing gowns from snowflakes, dewdrops, shreds of cloud, mist and spider threads.",

    {
      "id": "oblakinia-c01",
      "source_id": "oblakinia-s1",
      "quote": "Oblakinia, obłoczyca – serbsko-chorwacki demon słowiański; rodzaj wił.",
      "locator": "wstęp",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Oblakinia atau obłoczyca adalah demon Slavia dari tradisi Serbia-Kroasia, sejenis vila.",
        "en": "The oblakinia or obłoczyca is a Serbo-Croatian Slavic demon, a kind of vila."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "oblakinia-c02",
      "source_id": "oblakinia-s1",
      "quote": "Demon drobnej budowy, wzrostu połowy dorosłego człowieka. Proporcje ciała nie odbiegały od proporcji dorosłej kobiety. Cechowała ją spora smukłość, a poruszała się lekko, płynąc w powietrzu.",
      "locator": "opis",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Demon bertubuh kecil, setinggi separuh orang dewasa, dengan proporsi tubuh perempuan dewasa; ia sangat ramping dan bergerak ringan sambil melayang di udara.",
        "en": "A small demon, half the height of an adult, with the body proportions of a grown woman; she was very slender and moved lightly, floating through the air."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "oblakinia-c03",
      "source_id": "oblakinia-s1",
      "quote": "Twarz miała szczupłą, głowę z wysokim czołem, włosy proste, długie, koloru błękitnego. Oczy duże, z błękitnym spojrzeniem. Skórę miała niebieską.",
      "locator": "opis",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wajahnya tirus, dahinya tinggi, rambutnya lurus, panjang, dan berwarna biru langit; matanya besar dengan tatapan biru, dan kulitnya biru.",
        "en": "Her face was thin, her forehead high, her hair straight, long and sky-blue; her eyes were large with a blue gaze, and her skin was blue."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "oblakinia-c04",
      "source_id": "oblakinia-s1",
      "quote": "Głównym jej zajęciem było szycie sukien. Czyniła je z płatków śniegu, kropel rosy, strzępów obłoków, mgieł, nici pająków i ozdabiała je różnymi przedmiotami.",
      "locator": "opis",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pekerjaan utamanya menjahit gaun, yang ia buat dari kepingan salju, tetes embun, serpihan awan, kabut, dan benang laba-laba, lalu ia hiasi dengan berbagai benda.",
        "en": "Her main occupation was sewing gowns, which she made from snowflakes, dewdrops, shreds of cloud, mists and spider threads and decorated with various objects."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Praděd atau Altvater adalah roh gunung mitis, penguasa dan penjaga pegunungan Hrubý Jeseník, yang digambarkan sebagai lelaki tua berjanggut putih yang adil.",
    "en": "Praděd or Altvater is a mythical mountain spirit, ruler and guardian of the Hrubý Jeseník range, pictured as a just old man with a white beard.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "praded-c01",
      "praded-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Praděd, dalam bahasa Jerman Altvater, adalah roh gunung mitis yang menguasai dan menjaga pegunungan Hrubý Jeseník. Gunung tertinggi di kawasan itu, yang menurut legenda menjadi kediamannya, dinamai menurutnya; nama gunung Altvater sendiri baru tercatat pada abad ke-19. Ia paling sering digambarkan sebagai lelaki tua berjanggut putih panjang, tetapi juga muncul sebagai rimbawan atau gembala kecil.",
      "en": "Praděd, Altvater in German, is a mythical mountain spirit who rules and guards the Hrubý Jeseník mountains. The highest mountain of the region, said in legend to be his seat, is named after him; the mountain name Altvater itself is first recorded in the 19th century. He is most often pictured as an old man with a long white beard, but also appears as a forester or a shepherd boy.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "praded-c01",
        "praded-c02",
        "praded-c03",
        "praded-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut legenda ia adil, menghargai kebaikan, dan menunjukkan wajah garangnya kepada orang jahat. Ia pernah menghukum gembala muda Konrád yang tamak di ruang bawah tanah penuh harta, hingga anak itu sembuh dari ketamakan dan iri hati. Legenda setempat lain menyebutnya leluhur penghuni pegunungan yang tetap di dunia untuk melindungi keturunannya, tanpa kekuatan gaib, dan dikenal karena menolong kaum miskin.",
      "en": "In legend he is just, values goodness and shows his unfriendly face to the wicked. He once punished the greedy shepherd boy Konrád in an underground hall full of treasure, curing him of greed and envy. Another local legend calls him an ancestor of the mountain folk who stayed in the world to protect his descendants, without supernatural powers, known for helping the poor.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "praded-c03",
        "praded-c04",
        "praded-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Namanya kini dipakai untuk minuman keras herbal Altvater dan Praděd, dan pemahat Jiří Halouzka membuat patung Praděd setinggi sepuluh meter di Galeri Praděd di Jiříkov.",
      "en": "His name is now borne by the herbal liqueurs Altvater and Praděd, and the carver Jiří Halouzka made a ten-metre statue of Praděd for his Praděd Gallery in Jiříkov.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "praded-c02",
        "praded-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "praded-c01",
      "source_id": "praded-s1",
      "quote": "Praděd (německy Altvater) je mytický horský duch, vládce a strážce Hrubého Jeseník.",
      "locator": "úvod",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Praděd (bahasa Jerman: Altvater) adalah roh gunung mitis, penguasa dan penjaga pegunungan Hrubý Jeseník.",
        "en": "Praděd (German: Altvater) is a mythical mountain spirit, ruler and guardian of the Hrubý Jeseník mountains."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "praded-c02",
      "source_id": "praded-s1",
      "quote": "Je po něm pojmenovaná nejvyšší hora Hrubého Jeseníku, Moravy a českého Slezska Praděd a bylinné likéry Altvater a Praděd.",
      "locator": "úvod",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gunung tertinggi Hrubý Jeseník, Moravia, dan Silesia Ceko, yaitu Praděd, dinamai menurutnya, demikian pula minuman keras herbal Altvater dan Praděd.",
        "en": "The highest mountain of the Hrubý Jeseník, Moravia and Czech Silesia, Praděd, is named after him, as are the herbal liqueurs Altvater and Praděd."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "praded-c03",
      "source_id": "praded-s1",
      "quote": "Praděd je nejčastěji zobrazován jako stařec s dlouhým bílým vousem, ale zjevuje se i v jiných podobách, například jako lesník nebo pasáček. Podle legend je spravedlivý, oceňuje dobrotu a má spadeno na zlé lidi, kterým ukazuje svou nevlídnou tvář.",
      "locator": "Podoba",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Praděd paling sering digambarkan sebagai lelaki tua berjanggut putih panjang, tetapi juga muncul dalam wujud lain, misalnya sebagai rimbawan atau gembala kecil; menurut legenda ia adil, menghargai kebaikan, dan memusuhi orang jahat, yang ia tunjukkan wajah garangnya.",
        "en": "Praděd is most often pictured as an old man with a long white beard, but he also appears in other forms, such as a forester or a shepherd boy; according to legend he is just, values goodness and is set against wicked people, to whom he shows his unfriendly face."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "praded-c04",
      "source_id": "praded-s1",
      "quote": "Legenda vypráví například o tom, jak Praděd potrestal ovčáka, chlapce Konráda, kterého pozval do podzemní síně plné pokladů. Nakonec byl chlapec rád, když vyvázl s holým životem, a vůbec už nelitoval, že přišel o všechny dukáty, z chamtivosti a závistivosti byl vyléčen.",
      "locator": "Podoba",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebuah legenda menceritakan bagaimana Praděd menghukum gembala muda Konrád yang ia undang ke ruang bawah tanah penuh harta; akhirnya anak itu bersyukur bisa selamat, tidak menyesal kehilangan semua dukatnya, dan sembuh dari ketamakan serta iri hati.",
        "en": "A legend tells how Praděd punished the shepherd boy Konrád, whom he invited into an underground hall full of treasure; in the end the boy was glad to escape with his life, did not regret losing all his ducats, and was cured of greed and envy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "praded-c05",
      "source_id": "praded-s1",
      "quote": "Podle místní legendy je Praděd jeden z prapředků místních horalů, který neodešel na věčnost, ale zůstal na světě, aby chránil své potomstvo. Podle legend neměl žádné nadpřirozené schopnosti a do povědomí lidí se dostal tím, že pomáhal chudým, protože zbohatl a neměl žádné příbuzné.",
      "locator": "Podoba",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda setempat, Praděd adalah salah satu leluhur para penghuni pegunungan yang tidak pergi ke alam baka melainkan tetap di dunia untuk melindungi keturunannya; menurut legenda ia tidak punya kekuatan gaib dan dikenal orang karena menolong kaum miskin, sebab ia kaya dan tidak punya kerabat.",
        "en": "According to local legend, Praděd is one of the ancestors of the mountain folk who did not pass into eternity but stayed in the world to protect his descendants; legend says he had no supernatural powers and became known for helping the poor, since he had grown rich and had no relatives."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "praded-c06",
      "source_id": "praded-s1",
      "quote": "Jiříkovský řezbář Jiří Halouzka vytvořil desetimetrovou sochu Praděda, která je dominantou jeho Pradědovy galerie v Jiříkově.",
      "locator": "V kultuře",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pemahat kayu dari Jiříkov, Jiří Halouzka, membuat patung Praděd setinggi sepuluh meter yang menjadi ikon Galeri Praděd miliknya di Jiříkov.",
        "en": "The Jiříkov woodcarver Jiří Halouzka made a ten-metre statue of Praděd, the centrepiece of his Praděd Gallery in Jiříkov."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "praded-c07",
      "source_id": "praded-s1",
      "quote": "Název hory Altvater, česky Praděd, která je podle legend sídlem horského ducha, je poprvé zaznamenán v 19. století, předtím se pro ni používala řada jiných názvů.",
      "locator": "úvod",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama gunung Altvater (bahasa Ceko: Praděd), yang menurut legenda merupakan kediaman roh gunung itu, pertama kali tercatat pada abad ke-19; sebelumnya gunung itu disebut dengan berbagai nama lain.",
        "en": "The name of the mountain Altvater (Czech Praděd), said in legend to be the spirit's seat, is first recorded in the 19th century; before that various other names were used."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [

      "id": "Dalam mitologi Sami, rávgá adalah makhluk mirip manusia berambut panjang dan bermulut besar yang dijelaskan sebagai orang yang mati tenggelam.       "en": "In Sami mythology the rávgá is a humanlike being with long hair and a large mouth, explained as a drowned person.
